.nfl-root { --nfl-gap: 18px; }
.nfl-root table.nfl-table { width:100%; border-collapse:separate; border-spacing:0; font-size:12.5px; }
.nfl-root table.nfl-table th {
  padding:9px 14px; text-align:left; white-space:nowrap; font-size:10px;
  font-weight:700; letter-spacing:.06em; text-transform:uppercase;
  color:var(--text3); background:var(--bg3);
  border-bottom:1px solid var(--border); position:sticky; top:0; z-index:1;
}
.nfl-root table.nfl-table td {
  padding:8px 14px; white-space:nowrap; border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);
  vertical-align:middle; line-height:1.45;
}
.nfl-root table.nfl-table tbody tr:last-child td { border-bottom:none; }
.nfl-root table.nfl-table tbody tr:nth-child(even) { background:rgba(255,255,255,.018); }
.nfl-root table.nfl-table tbody tr:hover { background:rgba(255,255,255,.045); }
.nfl-root .nfl-num { font-family:var(--mono); font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums; text-align:right; }
.nfl-root .nfl-name { font-weight:600; color:var(--text); padding-right:20px; }
.nfl-root .nfl-pos {
  display:inline-block; min-width:34px; text-align:center;
  font-size:9.5px; font-weight:700; padding:2px 7px; border-radius:4px;
  background:var(--bg3); border:1px solid var(--border);
}
.nfl-root .nfl-scroll { overflow-x:auto; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:var(--r); }
.nfl-root .nfl-toolbar { display:flex; align-items:center; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap; margin-bottom:var(--nfl-gap); }
.nfl-root .nfl-card { border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:var(--r); overflow:hidden; margin-bottom:var(--nfl-gap); }
.nfl-root .nfl-card-head {
  padding:11px 16px; background:var(--bg3); border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);
  display:flex; align-items:center; justify-content:space-between; gap:12px; flex-wrap:wrap;
}
.nfl-root .nfl-card-title { font-weight:700; font-size:13px; color:var(--text); }
.nfl-root .nfl-section-label {
  font-size:10px; font-weight:700; letter-spacing:.08em; text-transform:uppercase;
  color:var(--text3); margin-bottom:10px;
}
.nfl-root .nfl-datebreak td { border-top:2px solid var(--border) !important; }
.nfl-root .nfl-panel {
  border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:var(--r);
  padding:16px 18px; margin-bottom:var(--nfl-gap); background:var(--bg2);
}
.nfl-root .nfl-srcgrid {
  display:grid; grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(165px,1fr));
  gap:8px 14px; margin:12px 0;
}
.nfl-root .nfl-srcgrid label {
  display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; font-size:12.5px;
  color:var(--text2); cursor:pointer; padding:3px 0;
}
.nfl-root .nfl-stat { padding:12px 18px; border:1px solid var(--border); border-radius:var(--r); }
.nfl-root .nfl-stat-label { font-size:9.5px; color:var(--text3); text-transform:uppercase; letter-spacing:.08em; margin-bottom:3px; }
.nfl-root .nfl-stat-val { font-family:var(--cond),var(--mono); font-size:23px; font-weight:700; line-height:1.1; }

  @import url('https://fonts.googleapis.com/css2?family=Inter:wght@300;400;500;600;700&family=JetBrains+Mono:wght@400;500;600&family=Barlow+Condensed:wght@500;600;700&display=swap');
  *, *::before, *::after { box-sizing: border-box; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
  :root {
    --bg:#070d14; --bg2:#0c1824; --bg3:#112030; --bg4:#162840;
    --border:#1a2e44; --border2:#1f3a54;
    --green:#00e87a; --green-dim:#009e53; --green-glow:rgba(0,232,122,0.15);
    --amber:#f5a623; --red:#f04c4c; --blue:#4db8ff; --purple:#a78bfa; --gold:#ffd700;
    --text:#ddeeff; --text2:#6b9ab8; --text3:#3a6480;
    --mono:'JetBrains Mono',monospace; --sans:'Inter',sans-serif; --cond:'Barlow Condensed',sans-serif;
    --r:6px; --r2:10px;
  }
  html,body,#root{height:100%;background:var(--bg);color:var(--text);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:14px;line-height:1.5;}
  ::-webkit-scrollbar{width:5px;height:5px} ::-webkit-scrollbar-track{background:transparent} ::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb{background:var(--border2);border-radius:3px}
  .app{display:flex;height:100vh;overflow:hidden}
  .sidebar{width:200px;flex-shrink:0;background:var(--bg2);border-right:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;flex-direction:column}
  .main{flex:1;overflow-y:auto}
  .sidebar-logo{padding:18px 16px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
  .logo-mark{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:18px;font-weight:700;color:var(--green);letter-spacing:.08em}
  .logo-sub{font-size:9px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:4px;font-family:var(--mono);letter-spacing:.12em}
  .sidebar-nav{flex:1;padding:10px 0;overflow-y:auto}
  .nav-group{padding:6px 12px 2px;font-size:8px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.14em;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;margin-top:6px}
  .nav-item{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:9px;padding:7px 14px;cursor:pointer;color:var(--text2);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;transition:all .12s;border-left:2px solid transparent;user-select:none}
  .nav-item:hover{background:var(--bg3);color:var(--text)} .nav-item.active{background:rgba(0,232,122,.07);color:var(--green);border-left-color:var(--green)}
  .nav-icon{font-size:14px;width:16px;text-align:center;flex-shrink:0}
  .sidebar-status{padding:10px 14px;border-top:1px solid var(--border)}
  .s-dot{display:inline-block;width:6px;height:6px;border-radius:50%;background:var(--green);margin-right:6px;vertical-align:middle}
  .s-dot.off{background:var(--red)} .s-dot.pulse{animation:pulse 2s infinite}
  @keyframes pulse{0%,100%{opacity:1}50%{opacity:.35}}
  .page-head{padding:22px 24px 0;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;gap:16px;flex-wrap:wrap}
  .page-title{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:22px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.04em;color:var(--text)}
  .page-sub{font-size:11px;color:var(--text3);margin-top:3px}
  .page-body{padding:16px 24px 32px}
  .card{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r2)}
  .card-head{padding:12px 16px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px}
  .card-title{font-size:10px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--text3)}
  .card-body{padding:14px 16px}
  .btn{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;gap:6px;padding:7px 14px;border-radius:var(--r);font-size:12.5px;font-weight:600;cursor:pointer;border:none;transition:all .12s;white-space:nowrap;font-family:var(--sans)}
  .btn-primary{background:var(--green);color:#050d14} .btn-primary:hover{background:#1fffa0;box-shadow:0 0 16px var(--green-glow)} .btn-primary:disabled{background:var(--green-dim);opacity:.5;cursor:not-allowed;box-shadow:none}
  .btn-ghost{background:transparent;color:var(--text2);border:1px solid var(--border2)} .btn-ghost:hover{background:var(--bg3);color:var(--text)}
  .btn-danger{background:transparent;color:var(--red);border:1px solid rgba(240,76,76,.3)} .btn-danger:hover{background:rgba(240,76,76,.1)}
  .btn-sm{padding:4px 10px;font-size:11.5px}
  .badge{display:inline-flex;align-items:center;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:99px;font-size:10.5px;font-weight:600;font-family:var(--mono);white-space:nowrap}
  .badge-green{background:rgba(0,232,122,.12);color:var(--green)} .badge-amber{background:rgba(245,166,35,.12);color:var(--amber)}
  .badge-red{background:rgba(240,76,76,.12);color:var(--red)} .badge-blue{background:rgba(77,184,255,.12);color:var(--blue)}
  .badge-purple{background:rgba(167,139,250,.12);color:var(--purple)} .badge-gold{background:rgba(255,215,0,.12);color:var(--gold)}
  .badge-gray{background:rgba(58,100,128,.2);color:var(--text2)}
  .g2{display:grid;grid-template-columns:1fr 1fr;gap:14px} .g3{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(3,1fr);gap:14px} .g4{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:14px}
  .flex{display:flex} .fcol{display:flex;flex-direction:column} .ac{align-items:center} .jb{justify-content:space-between}
  .g6{gap:6px} .g8{gap:8px} .g10{gap:10px} .g12{gap:12px} .g16{gap:16px} .fw{flex-wrap:wrap}
  .mt8{margin-top:8px} .mt12{margin-top:12px} .mt16{margin-top:16px} .mt20{margin-top:20px} .w100{width:100%}
  .log{background:#020810;border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r);font-family:var(--mono);font-size:11px;line-height:1.7;padding:12px;height:300px;overflow-y:auto;color:#4a7090}
  .log-line.ok{color:var(--green)} .log-line.warn{color:var(--amber)} .log-line.err{color:var(--red)} .log-line.info{color:var(--blue)} .log-line.agent{color:var(--purple)}
  .input{background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border2);border-radius:var(--r);color:var(--text);font-family:var(--sans);font-size:13px;padding:7px 11px;outline:none;width:100%}
  .input:focus{border-color:var(--green)}
  .flabel{font-size:10px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;display:block;margin-bottom:5px}
  .toggle{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:8px;cursor:pointer;user-select:none} .toggle input{display:none}
  .t-track{width:34px;height:18px;background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border2);border-radius:9px;position:relative;transition:background .2s;flex-shrink:0}
  .toggle input:checked+.t-track{background:var(--green-dim);border-color:var(--green)}
  .t-thumb{position:absolute;top:2px;left:2px;width:12px;height:12px;background:var(--text3);border-radius:6px;transition:transform .2s,background .2s}
  .toggle input:checked+.t-track .t-thumb{transform:translateX(16px);background:var(--green)}
  .t-label{font-size:12.5px;color:var(--text2)}
  .hr{border:none;border-top:1px solid var(--border);margin:12px 0}
  @keyframes spin{to{transform:rotate(360deg)}}
  .spin{width:14px;height:14px;border:2px solid var(--border2);border-top-color:var(--green);border-radius:50%;animation:spin .65s linear infinite;display:inline-block;flex-shrink:0}
  .empty{text-align:center;padding:48px 24px;color:var(--text3)} .empty-icon{font-size:36px;margin-bottom:10px;opacity:.5} .empty-msg{font-size:13px;line-height:1.6}
  .tabs{display:flex;gap:1px;padding:0 24px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);overflow-x:auto} .tabs::-webkit-scrollbar{height:0}
  .tab{padding:9px 14px;font-size:12.5px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;color:var(--text3);border-bottom:2px solid transparent;white-space:nowrap;transition:all .12s;user-select:none}
  .tab:hover{color:var(--text2)} .tab.active{color:var(--text);border-bottom-color:var(--green)}
  .run-item{display:flex;align-items:center;gap:10px;padding:9px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(26,46,68,.5)} .run-item:last-child{border-bottom:none}
  .setting-row{display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;padding:10px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(26,46,68,.5)} .setting-row:last-child{border-bottom:none}

  /* Pick cards */
  .picks-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(auto-fill,minmax(360px,1fr));gap:14px}
  .pick{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r2);overflow:hidden;transition:border-color .2s,box-shadow .2s}
  .pick:hover{border-color:var(--border2)} .pick.clickable{cursor:pointer} .pick.clickable:hover{border-color:var(--green);box-shadow:0 0 12px var(--green-glow)}
  .pick.gold-tier{border-color:rgba(255,215,0,.35);box-shadow:0 0 20px rgba(255,215,0,.06)}
  .pick.green-tier{border-color:rgba(0,232,122,.3);box-shadow:0 0 20px var(--green-glow)}
  .pick.amber-tier{border-color:rgba(245,166,35,.25)}
  .pick-top{padding:12px 14px;background:var(--bg3);display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
  .pick-rank{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:26px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);line-height:1;min-width:36px}
  .pick-name{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:18px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.02em;color:var(--text);line-height:1.1}
  .pick-matchup{font-size:11px;color:var(--text2);margin-top:3px;font-family:var(--mono)}
  .pick-conv{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:30px;font-weight:700;line-height:1;text-align:right}
  .pick-conv-label{font-size:8px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.1em;color:var(--text3);text-align:right}
  .pick-metrics{display:flex;gap:5px;padding:10px 14px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);flex-wrap:wrap}
  .metric{background:var(--bg3);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r);padding:5px 8px;min-width:50px;text-align:center}
  .metric-val{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:13px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text);line-height:1}
  .metric-label{font-size:8px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--text3);margin-top:2px}
  .pick-badges{padding:8px 14px;display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:4px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
  .pick-footer{padding:8px 14px;display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
  .odds-big{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:20px;font-weight:700;color:var(--amber)}
  .view-more{font-size:10px;color:var(--green);font-weight:600;letter-spacing:.04em}

  /* Hit picks */
  .hit-pick{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r2);overflow:hidden;display:flex;transition:border-color .2s,box-shadow .2s}
  .hit-pick.clickable{cursor:pointer} .hit-pick.clickable:hover{border-color:var(--green);box-shadow:0 0 12px var(--green-glow)}
  .hit-pick.scream{border-color:rgba(0,232,122,.3)}
  .hit-rank-col{background:var(--bg3);padding:10px 12px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;align-items:center;justify-content:center;min-width:52px;border-right:1px solid var(--border)}
  .hit-rank-num{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:22px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text3);line-height:1}
  .hit-score-big{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:15px;font-weight:600;color:var(--green);margin-top:4px}
  .hit-body{padding:10px 14px;flex:1}
  .hit-name{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:16px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)}
  .hit-matchup{font-size:11px;color:var(--text2);font-family:var(--mono);margin-top:2px}
  .hit-notes{font-size:11px;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.5;margin-top:6px}
  .grade-tag{font-size:10px;font-weight:600;padding:2px 7px;border-radius:4px;line-height:1.4}
  .grade-tag.elite{background:rgba(255,215,0,.12);color:var(--gold)} .grade-tag.nuclear{background:rgba(240,76,76,.15);color:#ff7070}
  .grade-tag.strong{background:rgba(0,232,122,.1);color:var(--green)} .grade-tag.lean{background:rgba(77,184,255,.1);color:var(--blue)}
  .grade-tag.tracking{background:rgba(167,139,250,.1);color:var(--purple)} .grade-tag.flag{background:rgba(245,166,35,.1);color:var(--amber)}
  .grade-tag.fade{background:rgba(240,76,76,.1);color:var(--red)}

  /* Modal */
  .modal-overlay{position:fixed;inset:0;background:rgba(4,8,16,.88);z-index:1000;display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:center;padding:24px;overflow-y:auto}
  .modal{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border2);border-radius:var(--r2);width:100%;max-width:820px;overflow:hidden;box-shadow:0 24px 80px rgba(0,0,0,.6)}
  .modal-head{padding:16px 20px;background:var(--bg3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:flex-start;justify-content:space-between;gap:12px;position:sticky;top:0;z-index:2}
  .modal-title{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:22px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)}
  .modal-sub{font-size:11px;color:var(--text2);margin-top:3px;font-family:var(--mono)}
  .modal-close{background:none;border:1px solid var(--border2);color:var(--text2);border-radius:var(--r);padding:5px 10px;cursor:pointer;font-size:13px;flex-shrink:0}
  .modal-close:hover{background:var(--bg4);color:var(--text)}
  .modal-body{padding:0}
  .modal-tabs{display:flex;gap:1px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--bg3);padding:0 20px}
  .modal-tab{padding:8px 14px;font-size:12px;font-weight:500;cursor:pointer;color:var(--text3);border-bottom:2px solid transparent;user-select:none;transition:all .12s}
  .modal-tab:hover{color:var(--text2)} .modal-tab.active{color:var(--text);border-bottom-color:var(--green)}
  .modal-section{padding:16px 20px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}
  .modal-section:last-child{border-bottom:none}
  .modal-section-title{font-size:9px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.12em;color:var(--text3);margin-bottom:10px}
  .detail-grid{display:grid;grid-template-columns:repeat(4,1fr);gap:8px}
  .detail-cell{background:var(--bg3);border-radius:var(--r);padding:8px 10px}
  .detail-cell-label{font-size:8.5px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.08em;color:var(--text3)}
  .detail-cell-val{font-family:var(--mono);font-size:14px;font-weight:600;color:var(--text);margin-top:3px}
  .notes-block{font-size:12px;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.7}
  .notes-line{padding:3px 0;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(26,46,68,.3)} .notes-line:last-child{border-bottom:none}
  .notes-line b{color:var(--text);font-weight:600}
  .notes-line.fire{color:var(--green)} .notes-line.warn{color:var(--amber)} .notes-line.info{color:var(--blue)} .notes-line.danger{color:var(--red)}

  /* Game conditions table */
  .cond-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11.5px}
  .cond-table th{padding:7px 10px;text-align:left;font-size:9px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.09em;color:var(--text3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--bg3);white-space:nowrap}
  .cond-table td{padding:8px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(26,46,68,.4);color:var(--text);vertical-align:middle}
  .cond-table tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none} .cond-table tr:hover td{background:rgba(17,32,48,.5)}

  /* AI picks */
  .ai-pick-card{background:var(--bg2);border:1px solid var(--border);border-radius:var(--r2);overflow:hidden;margin-bottom:10px}
  .ai-pick-header{padding:10px 14px;background:var(--bg3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;align-items:center;justify-content:space-between;gap:10px}
  .ai-pick-name{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:17px;font-weight:700;color:var(--text)}
  .ai-pick-meta{font-size:11px;color:var(--text2);font-family:var(--mono)}
  .ai-pick-body{padding:12px 14px;font-size:12.5px;color:var(--text2);line-height:1.65}
  .ai-pick-body b{color:var(--text);font-weight:600}
  .ai-rates{padding:8px 14px;border-top:1px solid var(--border);display:flex;flex-wrap:wrap;gap:6px}
  .ai-rate-item{background:var(--bg3);border-radius:var(--r);padding:3px 8px;font-family:var(--mono);font-size:10.5px;color:var(--green)}
  .ai-section-title{font-family:var(--cond);font-size:16px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.05em;color:var(--text);margin-bottom:12px;padding-bottom:8px;border-bottom:1px solid var(--border)}

  /* Grade table */
  .grade-table{width:100%;border-collapse:collapse;font-size:11.5px}
  .grade-table th{padding:7px 10px;text-align:left;font-size:9px;font-weight:700;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.09em;color:var(--text3);border-bottom:1px solid var(--border);background:var(--bg3)}
  .grade-table td{padding:8px 10px;border-bottom:1px solid rgba(26,46,68,.4);color:var(--text);vertical-align:top}
  .grade-table tr:last-child td{border-bottom:none} .grade-table tr:hover td{background:rgba(17,32,48,.5)}

  @media(max-width:900px){
    .g2,.g3,.g4{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .picks-grid{grid-template-columns:1fr}
    .detail-grid{grid-template-columns:repeat(2,1fr)}
    .app{flex-direction:column}
    .sidebar{display:none}
    .main{padding-bottom:70px}
    .bottom-nav{display:flex !important}
  }
  @media(min-width:901px){
    .bottom-nav{display:none !important}
  }
  .bottom-nav{
    position:fixed;bottom:0;left:0;right:0;height:60px;
    background:var(--bg2);border-top:1px solid var(--border);
    display:none;align-items:stretch;z-index:100;
    overflow-x:auto;-webkit-overflow-scrolling:touch;
  }
  .bottom-nav::-webkit-scrollbar{display:none}
  .bn-item{
    flex:1;min-width:60px;display:flex;flex-direction:column;
    align-items:center;justify-content:center;gap:3px;
    cursor:pointer;color:var(--text3);font-size:9px;font-weight:600;
    letter-spacing:.04em;text-transform:uppercase;padding:4px 2px;
    border-top:2px solid transparent;transition:all .12s;user-select:none;
    white-space:nowrap;
  }
  .bn-item.active{color:var(--green);border-top-color:var(--green);background:rgba(0,232,122,.06)}
  .bn-item:hover{color:var(--text2);background:var(--bg3)}
  .bn-icon{font-size:18px;line-height:1}
  .bn-label{font-size:8.5px;font-weight:700;letter-spacing:.05em;margin-top:1px;white-space:nowrap}
